# Decision log

Every architectural decision, with what triggered it and when to revisit. **Superseded
rows are kept deliberately so we do not re-litigate them.** Read this before proposing an
alternative — it may already have been rejected, with a reason.

---

| # | Date | Decision | Why | Status / revisit when |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **V1** | 20 Sep 2026 | **New codebase.** No code is carried in from anywhere | The factory architecture — GuideGraph, arm-don't-press, distillation — wants its own seams. Grafting it onto an existing shape costs more than laying them fresh | **Firm** |
| **V2** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Data and knowledge packs may be imported; nothing else may.** `data/knowledge`, `data/sources`, `data/guides`, the zone and name tables | Facts about TBC are expensive to assemble and cheap to verify. Code is the opposite | **Firm** — provenance in `data/README.md` |
| **V3** | 20 Sep 2026 | **JevRadio is pixel-encoded** — RGB grid, 4 bits/channel, calibration row, checksum plus sequence counter | Density and decode speed, at a robustness level that survives gamma and capture transforms | **Current** — revisit if organisers object to opaque addons; the fallback is legible digits read by a glyph atlas, which costs cells not correctness |
| **V4** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Claude is everything above System 1, over the subscription** (`claude -p`), **not the Anthropic API.** GLM is the cheap bulk rung | Owner has a subscription and no Anthropic key, and wants the ML takeover to be real rather than aspirational. Forces the wean onto the critical path, which is the correct place for it | **Current** — revisit if a hosted key appears, or if subscription limits block a gate |
| **V5** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The GuideGraph is generated from this server's world DB, then verified by walking** | 6,599 quests and 109,358 spawns with exact coordinates are already on disk. Generated *from* this server beats recorded *on* it. The recorder pass shrinks to routes, hazards and grind boxes — what only walking can answer | **Current** — `ARCHITECTURE.md` §8. Supersedes the hand-authoring mechanism in PLAN §7.6, not its intent |
| **V6** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Retail guide packs are a cross-check, never a source** | Custom servers delete, rename and relevel quests. Diffing pfQuest/RestedXP against the world DB is how those changes announce themselves | **Firm** |
| **V7** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Multi-client input path is deferred**; the architecture keeps it a deployment question | One cursor per Windows session is an OS constraint. Separate sessions, VMs, background input and dongles each cost differently, and one clean client beats ten bad ones for the learning goal | **Deferred** — decide before Gate B. Until then: no shared client state, `hid` backends swappable |
| **V8** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Rungs are named for their job, never their supplier** — `coach/`, `teacher/`, not `jev/jev/` or `claude/` | A vendor-named module forces a rename the moment the vendor moves, and that rename touches contracts | **Firm** |
| **V9** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Label by outcome, not by authorship.** Train only on choices graded good at +60 s | Cloning a teacher caps the student at the teacher's error rate, and we have ground truth. Promotes PLAN §12.1's optional bandit to load-bearing | **Firm** — `ARCHITECTURE.md` §2 |
| **V10** | 20 Sep 2026 | **`situation_key` in the schema from commit one** | It is simultaneously the teacher-dedup key, the counterfactual join, the agreement bucket and the answer cache. Unrecoverable if added late | **Firm** — bin widths versioned separately |
| **V11** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The teacher's preferred output is a durable artifact** — skill draft, graph patch, `on_fail` edge — not an immediate action | A decision helps one client once; a skill helps forever. On a rate-limited teacher that compounds. Inverts PLAN §9.2 | **Firm** |
| **V12** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The radio labels the vision heads.** Every `addon_ok` tick logs `(vision_estimate, radio_truth)` for every shared field | Makes fusion confidence measured rather than guessed, and makes addon-off a continuously validated mode rather than a hoped-for one | **Firm** — `ARCHITECTURE.md` §5 |
| **V13** | 20 Sep 2026 | **One source of truth for anything two components must agree on.** Python defines, Lua is generated | Agreement by convention decays silently; agreement by construction cannot | **Firm** — hand-editing `Fields.lua` is a build error |

| **V14** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The teacher's model is pinned, and pinned down: Sonnet, never the CLI default** | An unpinned `claude -p` inherits the user's default, which here is Opus — the scarcest model on the plan and the one the human is using to build with. A farm of ten clients would quietly consume the developer's own capacity. Measured: unpinned calls returned 429 "out of usage credits" while Sonnet and Haiku answered immediately; the *plan* was untouched, only the Opus allocation was spent, and the error message said neither | **Firm** — an explicit model still wins, for escalating a hard postmortem |
| **V15** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Wait for the artifact; discard the stale action** | Measured round trip on a realistic prompt is **52 s** against a 60 s situation bin, so a late answer is the common case. Artifacts describe the *step* and do not go stale; only the fallback action does, and by the time it lands the scripted coach has acted anyway. Discarding whole replies to avoid stale actions would throw away the durable half — which V11 says is the valuable half | **Firm** — `stale_after_s` on the runtime |
| **V16** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Outstanding questions are tracked by the bucket they were asked under** | `situation_key` bins step age, so a step crosses from "fresh" to "slow" at 60 s and its key changes mid-flight. Looking up only the *current* key misses every slow answer, re-asks, and never hits the cache the key exists to enable | **Firm** |

| **V17** | 20 Sep 2026 | **There is no facing API in 2.4.3. Heading is measured, not read** | `GetPlayerFacing` arrived in 3.0; on this client it does not exist, so the field decodes as unknown — confirmed live, and only our own addons reference it while the stock UI does not. Navigation therefore uses **closed-loop turning**: take a heading from a position delta while moving, compute the angle to the target, turn for `angle / turn_rate`, re-measure. Absolute facing is never needed, and a noisy reading costs another iteration rather than a walk into a lake | **Current** — V29 tried to supersede this and did not survive measurement; closed-loop turning is still how this bot aims |
| **V18** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The bot never fights the operator for the desktop** | `SetForegroundWindow` fails from a process that does not own the foreground — measured. `AttachThreadInput` is used so a supervisor can restore a window after a loading screen or a stray alt-tab, but `Hid` refuses to press anything whenever the window is not focused, and that guard is not conditional on it | **Firm** — the guard held on its first live test, refusing rather than typing into the wrong window |

| **V19** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The planner is a standalone Detour sidecar over the server's own mmaps.** `tools/jevpath` links the already-built `libDetour.a` and reads the already-extracted tiles; `MmapQuery` keeps one long-lived process per map | Those tiles are how CMaNGOS walks its own NPCs around Northshire Abbey — the exact problem — and they cost nothing because they were extracted when the server was built. Rejected: **in-process tiles** (no usable Detour binding for Python, and writing one is a project); **CMaNGOS `PathFinder`** (takes a `Unit*` and reaches into the game object model, so it cannot be called standalone — everything it adds over Detour is unit-specific smoothing); **the `.mmap path` GM command** (needs a logged-in character and a chat parser to read its own answer back); **building a second mesh** (a week, to reproduce what is on disk). Long-lived because map 0 is 513 tiles, 2.2 s and 577 MB to load and microseconds to query | **Current** — revisit if a leg needs a route the mesh does not have, which is what `RecordedQuery` is the seam for |
| **V20** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Waypoints are the route, so intermediate arrival is *tight*, not loose** | The obvious choice is wrong. Detour emits waypoints at polygon portals, which around a building come as close as **1.9 yards** apart, so an 8-yard "close enough" radius marked most of a route as already reached: the follower skipped ahead, cut the corner, and walked into the Abbey it had just been routed around — eight legs of nine, failing at the wall the planner existed to avoid | **Firm** — 3 yards, and waypoints closer than that are thinned rather than kept |

| **V21** | 20 Sep 2026 | **A heading is never taken across a turn, and the cruise deadband is 22 degrees** | A pulse arcs the character, so a heading window spanning the pulse measures the arc: the reading overshoots, the error flips sign, and the next tick corrects the other way. Measured: 50 turns in 37 s — a pulse every 0.7 s — on a three-point path a person would walk as two straight lines. Ten degrees is a docking tolerance; with the readout quantising at 0.12 yards and a heading needing 1.5 yards behind it, ten degrees is inside the noise on a long leg. After: **8 turns, 11.6 s, no stucks, no detours** | **Firm** — the weave was the controller, never the mesh |
| **V22** | 20 Sep 2026 | **A blocked planned leg re-queries the planner; it does not detour** | `_detour` is the wall heuristic for walking at a raw node. On a navmesh polyline it is the follower arguing with the planner, and it showed up as three detours on a route already solved. Unstick still runs — a fence is a fence — but the way round a building comes from the mesh, which knows about the door | **Firm** — `to(allow_detour=False)`, `follow(replan=...)` |

| **V23** | 20 Sep 2026 | **The quest log arrives one entry per paint** (`quests.slot` / `quests.count`), replacing a watched-quest field. Schema 2 | 2.4.3 has no "current quest" beyond the watch list and **nothing sets a watch**, so every quest field read as unknown on a live client that had quests in it — and the tracker could not answer "did I accept this?", which is the predicate the whole questing half of the guide rests on. Cycling needs no channel into the addon and keeps it stateless: the decoder reassembles the log from `slot`/`count` over ~2 s at 10 Hz, and `log_hash` says when to discard the assembly | **Current** — `count == 0` is a positive empty log, which is what puts a fresh character on `graph.entry` rather than on whoever is standing nearby |
| **V24** | 20 Sep 2026 | **A character `type_text` cannot send is a failure, not something to skip** | `/` was absent from the key table, so `slash("/reload")` typed **reload** — the addon was never reloaded, a schema bump appeared not to take, and the character announced itself to Northshire. Silent skipping turns a command into a chat message | **Firm** — unsendable characters are recorded and the call returns False |

| **V25** | 20 Sep 2026 | **One frame is never a quest log.** `to_state` returns `None` for any non-empty log; `jev.perceive.questlog.QuestLog` is the only thing that assembles one | The strip cycles one entry per paint, so a frame carries one quest out of `count`. Presenting that as a short log has the tracker find its step's quest absent and fire `QUEST_MISSING` — skipping a live step, twice a second, for as long as the log has more than one entry. Same class as `()` versus `None`, one layer further out. `count == 0` is the single case one frame settles: there are no slots to wait for | **Firm** — `log_hash` discards a half-built assembly rather than mixing two logs |

| **V26** | 20 Sep 2026 | **One locator for every unit on screen**: the selection ring and the nameplate, from one colour-and-components pass. `jev/perceive/units.py` | There is no way to ask 2.4.3 where a unit is, and aiming at a node cannot work because a node is a spawn point and units wander. The ring and plate bracket the model — feet and head — so the midpoint is the torso at any distance, for any unit, with no per-creature knowledge. Fill ratio separates them: a ring is hollow at 0.27, a bar solid at 0.97 | **Both anchors remain required** — measured NPC interactions work, but 22 September wolf frames pair real plates with yellow grass. Ring identity across terrain is not established. The earlier guessed fallback measured 1.27 on one frame and 2.05 on another and clicked Willem's feet |
| **V27** | 20 Sep 2026 | **`target.in_melee` gates nothing that needs interact range** | It is `CheckInteractDistance` index 3 — *duel* range, about eleven yards — while an NPC talks at roughly five. It reads true with the character standing well back, and hours went on right-clicking a model in plain sight and out of reach. Arrival is now the client's own collision: walk forward until the character stops moving | **Firm** — revisit only if a real ~5-yard check can be painted |
| **V28** | 20 Sep 2026 | **Skills get hard caps, and the caps are tested** | The first `Interact` grew a method per failure — preflight, approach, leave_range, turn_to, find_on_screen, locate, a click offset, a fourteen-point sweep — each a patch on the previous symptom. One `/target`, one look, at most one yaw, one click, one confirmation; `tests/test_interact.py` asserts the dead machinery is *absent* | **Firm** — a skill that needs a ninth step needs rewriting, not a ninth step |
| **V29** | 21 Sep 2026 | **Facing is readable in 2.4.3, off the minimap arrow** | The arrow the minimap draws is a `Model`, and `Model:GetFacing()` returns radians (XiconQoo/AVR-TBC, `Core.lua`). The addon polyfills `PLAYER_FACING` over it so `pos.facing` paints as it always claimed to, with **no schema change**. The arrow is matched on its model path, `Interface\Minimap\MinimapArrow`, because the frame name is not guaranteed. **Which object carries the angle is a client setting**: with `rotateMinimap` off the map is north-up and the arrow turns with the player; with it on the map turns and the arrow is pinned pointing up, so `Minimap:GetFacing()` is asked as well and the first to answer wins. This client has `rotateMinimap "1"`. Zero and sign are **measured, never reasoned**: `tools/verify_facing.py` walks forward from several headings and compares the painted value against the direction the character actually moved | **Tried and withdrawn** — measured on this client with `rotateMinimap 0` and a reload, `arrow:GetFacing()` returns **0.0 at every heading**: five walks, direction of travel spread across 160°, painted value unmoved. A constant is worse than nil because a navigator acts on it, so `pos.facing` paints unknown and **V17 stands**. The arrow may not be the model that carries the angle; settling that needs the addon to record what the minimap's children actually are into SavedVariables and read it off disk — client-local, no chat. Asking via `/script` is not an option: it types into the chat box, and a refused keystroke mid-line leaves half a command sitting in Say |

| # | Date | Decision | Why | Status / revisit when |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **V30** | 22 Sep 2026 | **One coach/tracker runtime, one input-owning worker.** The live probe delegates to `jev.run.cli`; `LiveBody` composes the previously measured body methods | The manual probe and simulated runtime had diverged: live traces lacked the guide, decision links and shadow evidence needed for learning. Tracking/recording must continue during long skills, while cancellation must release the old worker before another can press anything. Input and reader checkpoints plus bounded planner reads enforce the shared ownership seam | **Supervised Windows acceptance started** — concurrent recording, service composition and cooperative input release verified live; wolf engagement and a sustained soak remain pending |
| **V31** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Only applied decisions with observed outcomes become examples.** Preserve arm identity/duration, distinguish completion from rejoin, and exclude synthetic/replay sources by default | Rejected proposals could otherwise inherit the fallback's reward, short runs could earn credit without the full observation horizon, and repeated IDs across runs could cross-join. A failed skill cannot inherit a later retry's success. Missing evidence stays missing | **Implemented, verified offline** — promotion still requires representative live evidence |
| **V32** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Generated target identity is structured data.** Graph schema 2 adds creature/gameobject kind and exact name from the local server DB | Notes contain prose and mixed drop sources include gameobjects. Parsing notes or treating every placed objective as a creature silently targets the wrong thing. Existing route geometry and edges remain unchanged; unsupported target kinds and later unplaced objectives stop explicitly | **Implemented** — object locator and multi-target objective generation need their own evidence |

| **V33** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Merchant actions use painted stock buttons and confirmed transactions.** Schema 7 appends exact money, inventory, offers and supply observations; schema 6 retains explicit decoding | Full bags and empty supplies need the same Interact/list composition as quests. Only DB-confirmed, non-quest junk is sold; exact supported food/drink is bought. Inventory and money changes prove a transaction. The addon never sells, buys or presses anything | **Schema 7 installed; repair and water purchase verified live** — junk selling and wider vendor acceptance remain pending; no equipment destruction or speculative restocking |
| **V34** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Objective identity and coordinate frame are explicit.** Schema 3 carries every generated objective target, radio counter mapping, prerequisites and the graph's navigation frame | A later counter must not reuse the first creature's camp; a city map transition must not reinterpret fractions in the old region. Source zone facts are retained while navigation coordinates are normalized. An explicit supported-route derivative lists every exclusion and never rewards excluded quests | **Implemented, verified offline** — unknown targets, counters or coordinate transforms stay unknown; measured navigation methods remain unchanged |
| **V35** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Continuous learning is outcome-gated and revision-scoped.** Whole-run holdout, versioned artifacts, bounded canaries, fresh model-attributed outcomes, promotion and rollback | Agreement with historical actions is insufficient evidence of improvement. Missing coverage, synthetic windows, mixed controller credit and changed graph revisions cannot promote a model. Safety, recovery, combat and services retain the scripted floor | **Native Windows learner verified over 34 actual runs** — no errors, eligible examples or promotions; live improvement remains unproven |
| **V36** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Optional services never own input or block its clock.** Teacher calls and model loading run separately; input ownership, reconnect, durable playhead and watchdog stops stay in the shared supervisor | A subscription outage, slow model file or restart must not freeze the body or reset completed quests. Teacher proposals are retained, not installed as code. Actual quest/XP progress resets the watchdog; moving the playhead through retries does not | **Initial reconnect and cooperative stop verified on Windows** — teacher transport returned a valid reply, but requested model routing and the complete Windows soak remain pending |

| **V37** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Every fix must solve a failure class through a shared, scalable mechanism.** Task identities are data and acceptance cases, never control-flow patches | The owner explicitly rejects quest/NPC-specific fixes, accumulating fallback branches and duplicated implementations. Correct the owning contract across all callers, remove superseded paths, and verify representative cases. Different observed behavior contracts may have distinct composed skills; moving a workaround into configuration is not generalization | **Firm — explicit owner requirement.** `ARCHITECTURE.md` §6 and `docs/ROADMAP.md` apply this gate to every work package |

| **V38** | 22 Sep 2026 | **Jev teaches by playing bounded actions, then yields proven capabilities to a local student.** The guide and existing routines provide objectives and reusable skills; screenshots, controls, exact-server retrieval and recent outcomes inform each action | The owner clarified that direct teacher play and gradual weaning were the intended build. Whole-skill suggestions could not teach a correction to a failing approach. `jev/play` shares the existing input worker and verified primitives, records action and complete-episode outcomes, and evaluates independent runs before shadow/canary handover. Complete episode teacher cost and task throughput govern promotion; failures restore teacher control | **Implemented, verified offline.** Supersedes V11/V15's artifact-only restriction and the mandatory zero-teacher startup assumption in the original architecture. V35/V36 continue for scripted mode; teaching mode releases input during bounded model waits and stops on teacher unavailability. No live competence or promotion claimed; see `docs/TEACHING_LOOP.md` |

| **V39** | 22 Sep 2026 | **The visual teacher transport is explicitly selectable: Claude subscription or GLM vision API.** Both share the same bounded action and outcome contracts | After the Claude quota block, the owner supplied a GLM credential and requested a cheap model and live testing. The default GLM-4.6V-Flash is image-capable and officially listed free. Credentials stay outside Git and run configuration; one request is bounded, cancellable and never silently retried or routed to a paid model | **Current.** Extends V4 for the owner-authorized GLM API; whole-skill, motor-learning and execution semantics do not depend on the supplier |

| **V40** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Facing is turning until the selected unit's own nameplate is on the screen's centre line** (`Targeting.face_selected`), shared by Fight, the FACE_TARGET routine and the tutor. A right-click is not used to aim | Run `20260922T192105-3d5fcf` frames 99-104: after a hover-verified right-click the Attack slot flashed (auto-attack on) while the wolf stood two yards away at the character's side at full health. A right-click starts an attack but never turns the character, so every stride walked a heading nothing had set. The camera sits behind the character, so a unit on the centre line is straight ahead at any distance; selecting one unit fades the other plates, so the bright plate is the target's. Each turn pulse measures the rate it actually produced | **Current** — implemented and tested offline against a simulated world with real plate pixels; needs live confirmation that swings land |
| **V41** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Melee auto-attack is pressed only when observed off.** Schema 9 paints `bars.attacking` (stock Attack-button flash: `IsAttackAction` + `IsCurrentAction`), `target.melee_range` (`IsActionInRange` on Attack) and a UI error held 1.5 s with a counter | The rotation pressed the Attack toggle after the right-click had already started the swing, switching it off. ARCHITECTURE §6 forbids a toggle without its observed state; the radio had no such state. Closing now stops exactly when a swing reaches; a new "facing the wrong way" error re-faces at once | **Current** — Lua executed under the stub; schema 6-8 captures still decode. Needs the addon deployed to the client |
| **V42** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Corpses are clicked where a fresh hover reports the selected unit dead** (`Targeting.click_corpse`), probing under the last plate the unit had alive, then the centre line. The ring-only corpse path is removed | A dead unit has no nameplate, so the nameplate ambiguity that forbids trusting a living unit's hover cannot occur; corpses do not move. Ring detection is what fails on grass. No live corpse click had ever been recorded | **Current** — offline only; the first live kills are its acceptance |
| **V44** | 23 Sep 2026 | **A plate is the target's only when exact hover ownership proves it; it is then tracked.** Kills are proved by health reaching zero or by experience as the target vanishes; a deselected corpse is found by a dead hover of the killed unit's name | Live, 23 Sep: selection did not fade a rabbit's plate; a damaged unit's plate shrank below the shape test; the client cleared the selection at the moment of a kill (XP arriving with it). Brightness, whole-bar shape and a persisting selection were all assumptions the first facing-based kill disproved | **Current** — the kill is measured; corpse-by-name and plate tracking need their first live repetitions |
| **V43** | 23 Sep 2026 | **The tutor replies with one action name from a state-built menu plus flat parameters.** Capability and expected effect are derived locally; the prompt is compact text; transient provider errors are retried with backoff inside the deadline; one corrective re-ask; an unavailable tutor hands the objective to the scripted routine | The first supervised attempt stopped before any action: the nested union and two redundant labels failed validation on every free-model reply, from a 28k-token prompt. Replays of the same scenes now return valid actions (escape on the login dialog, COMBAT_PROFILE beside a wolf) from about 3k tokens. The free tier answers "overloaded" (1305) in bursts; stopping on it would end every run | **Current** — supersedes V38's reply format, not its learning contract. The Z.AI coding-plan endpoint is not used: its terms restrict it to supported coding tools |

| **V45** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Acquisition looks round before Tab, and a plateless Tab pick is located by the selection it draws.** Three quarter turns for a hover-proved plate of the wanted unit; then Tab; a pick with no plate is turned toward by `units.selection_marks` (selection colour new between the frames either side of the key) and walked at, and never walked at without a mark. A selection kept from before a fight is dropped when its plate is not on screen | Plates are drawn only within about twenty yards and the camera shows about a hundred degrees: 43 of 52 fights in run `20260923T100704-98a757` found nothing, while one stale far pick absorbed 28 fights in the run before. Four blind walks at plateless picks found none | **Current** — look-round verified live (empty looks 43 -> 29, no unseen fights); the mark locator is tested offline and not yet exercised live |
| **V46** | 23 Sep 2026 | **A living unit is clicked under its plate when its ring is hidden** (`units.body_candidates`): points at least 12 px below the bar, inside its span, clear of other bars, still authorized only by a fresh owning hover | Eagan Peltskinner stood just beyond the character with his plate clear and his ring hidden by the character's own model; the turn-in failed on "no eligible target geometry". A point below the bar cannot be the nameplate, so the hover that owns the target there is its body | **Current** — verified live: the next session's click opened his quest frame at the first body point |
| **V47** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Quest reward choice is a painted default, overrulable.** Schema 10 paints the choice count, whether one is chosen, and the default's position (usable, then quality, then the earlier item); the advance routine clicks it first and the tutor has `quest_reward` | Complete Quest does nothing until a reward is chosen; three presses on "Wolves Across the Border" changed nothing. The addon still actuates nothing: it reports the stock panel as it reports the advance button | **Current** — verified live: quest 33 handed in with Soft Fur-lined Shoes. Ranking rewards stays a decision the default does not claim |
| **V48** | 23 Sep 2026 | **A body that plays an objective in stages keeps its routine's clock.** While the tutor plays, the teaching episode's own bounds apply; when a scripted routine takes over, the catalog budget starts then. The tutor's transient retries fill its 30 s deadline while a reply can still arrive | A tutor spent 25 s retrying an overloaded provider and the scripted accept, timed from the arm, timed out walking to Marshal McBride. The previous 2/4/6 s retry schedule left 13 of 30 s unused during four consecutive refusals | **Current** — verified live: the next session's scripted accept completed after a tutor fallback |
| **V49** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Falling interrupts a skill only after 2.5 s; a re-plan starts at the height of the route being followed** | Every unstick jump read as falling (0.5-2.05 s measured) and the cancelled jump kept the character on the near side of a fence for 45 s. On Northshire Abbey's back ledge, a start at the destination's floor height snapped through the wall and every re-plan was a straight line into it (27 stuck events) | **Current** — the grace is verified live; the re-plan height is verified against the real navmesh at all five recorded ledge positions, not yet live |

| **V50** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Heals are cast on the caster, and a spell waiting for a target is observed and cancelled.** Heal-role abilities use the self-cast modifier (the saved `modifiedclick ALT SELFCAST`); schema 11 paints `SpellIsTargeting()` as `bars.targeting`; `Targeting.cancel_pending_spell` presses Esc only on that observation, and the tutor may only observe or Esc while it holds | Reported by the operator and found in frames 534-543 of `20260923T110010-adc4a3`: a between-fights Holy Light with a corpse selected waited for a click, its button lit, and three fights selected nothing because every selection click tried to cast it | **Current** — offline; schema 11 is installed and loads at the next login |
| **V51** | 23 Sep 2026 | **The visual tutor uses the Claude subscription: `claude-opus-4-7` at medium effort** (`--teacher-effort`, the CLI's `--effort`). GLM is no longer the configured tutor | Owner's choice after five sessions in which the free GLM tier refused most requests. Measured on saved frames: 5.7 s smoke, 6.4 s decision replay. Calls draw on the same subscription as the owner's own sessions | **Current** — supersedes V39's provider default for teaching; not yet exercised in a live session |
| **V52** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Walking learns where the mesh is wrong.** A leg blocked until the follower's own detour got past records the spot and the widest point of that escape (`RouteMemory`, `var/route-memory.json`); every later route passing the spot goes by that point. A re-plan that returns the same blocked route detours at once | The planner's navmesh is the server's creature mesh: one polygon spans both sides of a Northshire rail fence, and marking stuck spots expensive changed none of three recorded routes. Only the character's own experience locates those spots. Simulated with the real `Travel` (`walk_sim`): a 60-yard fence 42.4 s -> 11.0 s on the second trip, a trunk beside a wall 20.5 s -> 8.8 s, both with no stuck event | **Current** — simulation and offline only; the first live trips build the memory |

| **V53** | 23 Sep 2026 | **Closing to melee is one steered walk that stops at the first resolved swing.** Schema 12 counts the character's own resolved swings from the combat log (`combat.swings`, hits and misses); forward is held while the tracked plate is steered on; past `in_melee` the walk is bounded and a charging unit is met; reach expires after 4.5 s without a swing | The operator watched the stride loop stutter (walk, stand, walk, stand, nudge) because every stride waited on a full facing look and reach was known only from damage. Simulated with the real `Fight`: 25 yards closed in 2.9 s with forward pressed once, stopping at 4.7 yards | **Current** — simulation and offline; schema 12 installed, loads at the next login |

| **V54** | 23 Sep 2026 | **The client gets the addon as one anonymous file that owns no globals.** The sources are module bodies returning tables; `tools/gen_addon_fields.py` builds `StatusStrip/StatusStrip.lua` (each part in its own function scope, comments stripped) under the title "Status Strip" with no author; `--install` moves earlier installs aside. The Lua tests run the built file, and the harness fails any run that sets a global or names a frame | The operator's anti-cheat review: the event runs a custom Warden, and the addon exposed eight distinctive globals, a title naming the project and its own comments to any Lua-global or memory-signature check, from a public repository. ### Standing constraint
**Windows has one system cursor per session.** No input method — `SendInput`, a dongle,
anything — lets the bot play while you use the same desktop. See V7.

### Carried over from the plan, unchanged
No DLL injection, no memory read/write, no packet forge, no speed or teleport. Control is
virtual HID; sense is screen capture plus an addon that paints and never actuates. Offline
server only. (PLAN §2.)
